CREATE TABLE Books (id Int IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY ,name NVarChar (1500) ,comment NVarChar (500) ,logo NVarChar (300) ,parentId Int ,addDate DateTime DEFAULT getDate(),detail NText ,isHide Bit DEFAULT 0,authorName NVarChar (150) ,ISBN NVarChar (150) ,price Real ,pagesCount Int );
CREATE TABLE Folders (id Int IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY ,name NVarChar (150) ,comment NVarChar (500) ,logo NVarChar (300) ,parentId Int FOREIGN KEY REFERENCES Folders (id),addDate DateTime DEFAULT getDate(),isHide Bit DEFAULT 0);

CREATE TABLE messagesBox (id Int IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY ,name NVarChar (150) ,phone NVarChar (150) ,address NVarChar (300) ,email VarChar (75) ,subject NVarChar (300) ,messageText NText ,sendDate DateTime DEFAULT getDate(),isRead Bit DEFAULT 0,isHide Bit DEFAULT 0,link NVarChar (300) );
CREATE TABLE ReplaysBox (id Int IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY ,email VarChar (75) ,subject NVarChar (300) ,message NText ,sendDate DateTime DEFAULT getDate());
CREATE TABLE NoProgsBox (id Int IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY ,name NVarChar (150) ,tone NVarChar (150) );

CREATE TABLE Courses (id Int IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY ,name NVarChar (1500) ,comment NVarChar (500) ,logo NVarChar (300) ,parentId Int ,addDate DateTime DEFAULT getDate(),detail NText ,isHide Bit DEFAULT 0,level NVarChar (300) ,period NVarChar (300) ,price Real );
CREATE TABLE Sculpts (id Int IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY ,name NVarChar (1500) ,comment NVarChar (500) ,logo NVarChar (300) ,parentId Int ,addDate DateTime DEFAULT getDate(),detail NText ,isHide Bit DEFAULT 0,artist NVarChar (300) );

CREATE TABLE units (id Int IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY ,name NVarChar (150) ,description NVarChar (600) ,factor Float DEFAULT 1,image NVarChar (600) );
INSERT INTO units (name,description,factor,image) VALUES (N'ل.س',N'ليرة سورية',1,'NULL') SELECT @@IDENTITY
